Heated Debates on Police & Lake Rules

Thursday, May 7, 2015 Austin City Council Regular Meeting
  • A highly contentious debate surrounded the nomination of a police accountability activist to the Public Safety Commission, ultimately failing due to a denied residency waiver.
  • Council discussed interim changes to marine toilet regulations for boats on Lady Bird Lake, balancing water quality concerns with the need for current operators to continue service.
  • Policies for granting residency waivers for city commission appointments were deliberated, weighing geographic representation against the value of specialized expertise.
  • Concerns were raised regarding the civility and respectful conduct expected of nominees, emphasizing the importance of productive discourse on city boards.
Official City Record Agenda Watch Video Transcript

[1:16:17 PM] It's $2 out of every policy to that pool of money. So of these -- of that pool, 29 grantees, Austin police department's auto theft interdiction project is one of them. They only help with administration of the office and moving cases along and things like that.
